
Current true run: `12` PrefabBinder variables, `26` MonoBehaviour components, `6` TextEx components, `0` errors, and `4` semantic edges from `selflabelchatcontent.bytes` / `otherlabelchatcontent.bytes`. Main conclusion: both self/other chat message prefabs bind variable id `1` as `labelContent:TextEx`, id `2` as `CalcTxt:TextEx`, id `5` as `AllianceCheck:TextEx`, and the Lua code's `SetComponent(LuaTextEx, 1/2/5)` lines match that prefab order. `AllianceCheck` has an explicit Lua `AddHyperClickEvent`; `labelContent` does not. PrefabBinder's serialized data only carries variable id, alias, GameObject reference, and type name, so the missing `labelContent` href callback is not hidden in these two prefabs.

The chat-label href registration-gap follow-up now has a separate static-exclusion report:

```text
apk_static_index/hot_update_gongfa_homemake_share_href_registration_gap_evidence.tsv

apk_static_index/hot_update_gongfa_homemake_share_href_registration_gap_report.md
```

Current true run: `81` evidence rows and `8` semantic edges. Main conclusion: `TextEx.OnPointerClick` requires `clickHyperCallId >= 1` before dispatching `CallBackManager.CallStringDelegate`, while `LuaTextEx:AddHyperClickEvent` is the only visible Lua API that writes that id through `TextExBridge.AddHyperClickListener`. `LuaUIText:SetText/SetTextSimple` only forwards text, `ChatContentBase.DoUpdateContent` only instantiates/binds/refreshes content, `ChatLabelChatCell.RefreshData` only sets `labelContent` text plus long-press copy, and direct exported Lua bridge bypasses were not found. Therefore the current static evidence does not prove chat message body clicks are wired; if the live game can click these labels, the missing registration is likely runtime/generated/non-exported behavior. Otherwise treat `66|createTime` in chat labels as rich display/copy markup with a reusable backend consumer, not necessarily a body-click entry.

API for rerun:

Current true run outputs `6` frame rows, `7` primitive rows, `12` evidence rows, all found, with `5` `ReadInt` calls in evidence. Verdict: `supports_raw_socket_decoder_outline=true`. Static outline: outer receive uses 4-byte `ByteUtil.ReadInt` length; send symmetry is `WriteNoCompress(totalLength) -> headerStream(sn, proId) -> bodyStream -> BeginSend`; receive constructs `LusuoStreamQuick(..., isMsgCompress)`, reads a forced non-compressed first int, then normal ints for the inner header before `PoolMessageManage.IsLuaMessage/read`. `ReadIntCompress` is varint/zigzag-like but still needs a packet fixture for final byte-perfect reimplementation.

Candidate codec: `u=((value<<1)^(value>>31))&0xffffffff`, then 7-bit varint continuation bytes; decode with `(u>>1) ^ -(u&1)`. This is executable and self-consistent with static `WriteIntCompress/revertSign32/ReadInt` evidence, but remains a candidate until a real packet fixture confirms the exact byte stream.

It joins `DoupoTowerDefense.CharacterMainInfo / CharacterSkillShow / CharacterSkillInfo / CharacterSkillStrength / CharacterLevel` with `DoupoCardCompose.ComposeCard / ComposeType / DrawCard / ComposeProgress / ComposeBook / ComposeCardQuality`, plus `lang.lua` and item names when available. Current true run indexes `11` partners, `120` compose cards, `22` skill-show rows, `69` skill-logic rows, `69` runtime-enriched skill rows, `88` strengthen rows, `2420` level rows, `4` draw-card rows, `4` compose-progress rows, `74` compose-book rows, and `11` quality rows. Catalog schema version `2` also expands partner-level `draw_sources`, `compose_quality_sources`, `compose_progress_rewards`, `compose_book_entries`, and `logic_skills[].runtime`, so the wiki can show item costs, weighted card pools, chance text, attached rewards, book/source links, timeline ids, buff rows, and `DoupoTDBuff*` semantic flags instead of only static card档位. `/fanxiu/wiki` renders this as a searchable `斗破角色` tab with partner overview, rich skill text, in-battle skills, `技能逻辑`, `来源与概率`, quality/star card档位, strengthen unlocks, and config summary. The adjacent skill-timeline probe links the `69` `CharacterSkillInfo` rows to `46` doupotd timeline configs and `26` runtime effect classes through `DoupoTDSkillActor -> DoupoTDBaseSkill:AddSkillEffectClassPath -> DoupoTDEffectType.EffectClass`; all requested timelines are found, and `20` ids have exact-name collisions where the useful doupotd export is a hash-suffixed file rather than `<id>.lua`. The BuffEffect probe links `19` skill rows / `21` skill-buff usages / `13` direct buff ids plus `4` one-hop secondary buff ids into `17` `BuffEffect` rows and `11` `DoupoTDBuff*` runtime classes through `DoupoTDType.BuffPath`; all direct ids resolve and all selected classes have visible Lua files. The Buff class semantics probe summarizes those `11` classes into `82` function signatures and `152` evidence lines; `3` classes read/emit trigger buff ids, `1` class directly touches timeline ids, and `10` classes mutate runtime buff state. The Buff class flow probe now expands `10` high-value classes into `77` Lua functions and `381` categorized control-flow steps; it identifies `4` classes with `AddBuff`, `9` with `RemoveBuff`, `3` with random trigger gates, `2` with skill filters, `3` with target-buff checks, and `2` with friend-target expansion. `doupotd_catalog.json` now embeds this as `logic_skills[].runtime.buffs[].flow`, and the wiki `技能逻辑` panel renders `flow_hint`, flow chips, and key function chips. Important hints: `DoupoTDBuffHitAddBuff` filters by `skillGroup/skillType` and target existing buffs before random-gated `triggerBuffId` application, while `DoupoTDBuffMingxin` applies friend buffs on start and removes them at duration end. The Buff authority-boundary probe compares those client-side Buff flows with visible doupotd protocol/schema evidence: true run covers `10` flow classes, `58` protocol packets, `11` relevant packets, `0` buff-like protocol fields, `120` Lua evidence rows, `5` `AddDamageResult` calls, `16` `AddBuff` calls, `54` `RemoveBuff` calls, and `11` `GamePlayer` evidence rows. Static verdict: visible Buff flow is client-local, visible doupotd protocol has game progress summaries such as `CM_DoupoTDGamePlayer(currWave/wavePercent/killNum/bossVoList)` and `SM_DoupoTDGamePlayer(finishWave/rewardResults/passLevelVOS/levelId/gameType/isSkipLevel/wavePercent)`, but no visible per-Buff state packet in the doupotd schema. The Effect/GamePlayer summary object probe then expands that boundary into `19` packet fields, `5` nested object fields, and `46` evidence rows: `SM_DoupoTDEffect.effectVO -> DoupoTDEffectVO(index/type/id)`, where `type` is interpreted by visible Lua as `1` role acquisition, `2` `DoupoTowerDefense_SkillEnhance`, and `3` `DoupoTowerDefense_TurnSkillEnhance`; `CM_DoupoTDGamePlayer.bossVoList` and `CM_DoupoTDRefreshWave.bossVoList` both point to `DoupoBossVo(id/hp)`, and `hp` is computed as `currentHp / maxHp * 10000`. The GamePlayer result probe then expands the result-display boundary into `17` message fields and `36` evidence rows: `rewardResults` is a server-returned `RewardResult(type/code/amount/content/mail/isFirstGet/additions/extraMark)` list consumed by `CostAndRewardMgr:AddRewardResults` and the result view item scroller, while `passLevelVOS` is read with `MessageList2List` but written and consumed as an int-list of passed level ids (`passLevelVOS:Contains(levelId)` and `InitNewLevelDic(passLevelVOS)`). Validation after this integration: `uv run pytest backend/tests/test_fanxiu_resources.py -q` is `161 passed`, current Fanxiu resource route count is `195`, and `npm run typecheck --prefix frontend` plus `npm run build --prefix frontend` pass from the prior wiki-flow integration. This is static readable catalog/client-effect/buff-dispatch/result-display data; protocol authority for draw/compose/upgrade and final combat formulas remains the doupotd NetLogic reports or deeper runtime/server evidence.

Key files are `DigitDoorFightComponent.lua`, `DigitDoorEntityMgr.lua`, `DigitDoorData.lua`, `DigitDoorMgr.lua`, `DigitDoorNetLogic.lua`, and `DigitDoorBaseSkill.lua`. `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_netlogic_flow_report.md` slices `25` NetLogic functions with `24` field assignments and `103` edges. `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_protocol_schema_report.md` joins `22` packet schemas / `52` field rows and now has `assigned_fields_align=true`: `7` client requests match schema, while `CM_DigitDoorReadyFight.levelId` and `CM_DigitDoorUnlock.Id` remain visible schema fields with no static assignment in current sends. Tooling note: `backend/core/fanxiu_lua_packet_index.py` now resolves request field ownership through `GetMessageFromPools(_ActualPacket)` instead of trusting local variable names; this fixes the misleading `CM_DigitDoorRefDoorFun` alias case where the local variable is named `CM_DigitDoorUpLevel` but the pooled packet is `_CM_DigitDoorRefDoor`. Superseding validation: `uv run pytest backend/tests/test_fanxiu_resources.py -q` is `164 passed`, `uv run python -m py_compile backend\core\fanxiu_lua_packet_index.py backend\core\fanxiu_apk_static.py backend\api\fanxiu_resources.py` passes, and current Fanxiu resource route count remains `202`.

Additional digitdoor ReadyFight pair-flow update: `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_cm_digitdoorreadyfight_sm_digitdoorreadyfight_pair_flow_report.md` now drills `CM_DigitDoorReadyFight -> SM_DigitDoorReadyFight`. True run joins `2` packet rows, `9` packet fields, `4` flow edges, and `74` source rows; checks `protocol_schema_confirmed`, `netlogic_flow_confirmed`, request/response joins, source rows, and flow edges all pass. The request packet declares `levelId`, but visible `CM_DigitDoorReadyFightFun` only obtains `_CM_DigitDoorReadyFight` from the message pool and sends it, with no static `levelId` assignment. The response is the scene-ready snapshot: `SM_DigitDoorReadyFightFun -> DigitDoorMgr:DigitDoorReadyFright(msg)`, then `DigitDoorData:DigitDoorReadyFright` writes `levelId -> SetGameLevel`, `wave -> SetWave`, `gameType -> SetGameType`, `skillList -> SetCouncilSkill2lvMap`, `indexList -> SetFightPartnerVoList`, and raises `DigitDoorType.EventType.IniDataFinish`; `DigitDoorMgr` also has a reconnect-in-game guard and raises `SceneInfoSync` when entering fresh. Keep `CM_DigitDoorReadyFight.levelId` as a source-placement/static-fill gap, not proof the field is impossible.

Additional digitdoor Unlock pair-flow update: `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_cm_digitdoorunlock_sm_digitdoorunlock_pair_flow_report.md` now drills `CM_DigitDoorUnlock -> SM_DigitDoorUnlock`. True run joins `2` packet rows, `2` packet fields, `5` flow edges, and `62` source rows; all pair-flow checks pass. The request packet declares `Id`, but visible `CM_DigitDoorUnlockFun` only gets `_CM_DigitDoorUnlock` from the message pool and sends it, with no static assignment and no visible call site found by broad source search. The response packet carries `list`; on `msg.code == 0`, `DigitDoorData:UpdateDDPartnerVos(msg.list)` refreshes character VOs through `UpdateOneDigitDoorCharacterVo -> DigitDoorCharacterVo:SetServerData`, `DigitDoorMgr:OpenDigitDoorNewCharacterView(msg.list,true)` records newly opened `v.id` values and may show `Window.DigitDoorNewCharacterView`, then `Update_Character_Info` is raised. Treat this as server-returned unlock/character-state refresh evidence; the client-visible Lua does not expose the unlock selection/source placement.

Additional digitdoor GamePlayer pair-flow update: `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_cm_digitdoorgameplayer_sm_digitdoorgameplayer_pair_flow_report.md` drills `CM_DigitDoorGamePlayer -> SM_DigitDoorGamePlayer`. True run joins `2` packet rows, `10` packet fields, `12` flow edges, and `114` source rows; all pair-flow checks pass and the client request fields fully match schema. The client sends progress snapshot fields `currWave`, `wavePercent`, `killNum`, and `bossVoList`: `killNum` is `totalKillMonsterNum - skillMonsterNum - totalKillBossNum`, and each `DDBossVo(id,hp)` reports boss hp as `Mathf.Floor(currentHp / maxHp * 10000)` when the boss entity is visible. The server response carries `finishWave`, `rewardResults`, `passLevelVOS`, `levelId`, `gameType`, and `isSkipLevel`; `DigitDoorExitGame` stores skip state and calls `DigitDoorData:SetFinishLevelInfo(msg)`, which only rebuilds passed-level state from `passLevelVOS`. Non-skip completion opens `DigitDoorResultInfoView(msg)`, whose win/fail display is driven by whether `passLevelVOS:Contains(msg.levelId)` and whose reward display is `msg.rewardResults`. Skip completion shows `rewardResults` in the reward pop, resets `isSkipLevel`, then opens newly unlocked character UI from recorded ids. Treat this as the main DigitDoor battle settlement boundary: client contributes progress/boss-hp summary, while pass/reward/final display authority is server-returned.

Additional digitdoor StartGame pair-flow update: `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_cm_digitdoorstartgame_sm_digitdoorstartgame_pair_flow_report.md` drills `CM_DigitDoorStartGame -> SM_DigitDoorStartGame`. True run joins `2` packet rows, `3` packet fields, `5` flow edges, and `26` source rows; all checks pass. The request field `indexList` is filled by iterating `DigitDoorData:GetFightPartnerVoList()`, which is maintained by `DigitDoorSceneRoleItem` clicks and `DigitDoorFightComponent` drag/drop through `UpdateFightPartnerVoList(id,hole/index)`. `DigitDoorSceneView` requires this list to be non-empty before `ReqStartGame`, then calls `CM_DigitDoorStartGameFun`. The response schema declares `indexList` and `skillVos`, but the visible `SM_DigitDoorStartGameFun` does not consume those fields; it only checks the code and calls `DigitDoorMgr:DigitDoorStartGame(msg)`, which sets `V_StartGame=true` and raises `OnStartGame`. `DigitDoorSceneView` consumes `OnStartGame` by hiding the pre-battle lineup UI, refreshing camera/type, calling `DigitDoorEntityMgr:StartGame()`, and enabling role drag.

Additional digitdoor RefreshWave pair-flow update: `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_cm_digitdoorrefreshwave_sm_digitdoorrefreshwave_pair_flow_report.md` drills `CM_DigitDoorRefreshWave -> SM_DigitDoorRefreshWave`. True run joins `2` packet rows, `6` packet fields, `7` flow edges, and `49` source rows; all checks pass. The client request sends `wave`, `addExp`, `killNum`, and `bossVoList`: `addExp` is statically set to `LusuoLong.FromNumber(0)`, while `killNum` and `bossVoList` reuse the same local battle summary helpers as GamePlayer. `DigitDoorEntityMgr` calls this during next-wave checks, reconnection while waiting for next-wave data, and final-wave completion checks. The server response carries `refreshWave` and `currExp`; visible Lua consumes only `refreshWave`, writes `DigitDoorData:SetWave(msg.refreshWave)`, and raises `DigitDoorType.EventType.RefreshWave` for scene/entity refresh. Treat this as a mid-run progress synchronization boundary, not a reward-settlement boundary.

Additional digitdoor UpLevel pair-flow update: `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_cm_digitdooruplevel_sm_digitdooruplevel_pair_flow_report.md` drills `CM_DigitDoorUpLevel -> SM_DigitDoorUpLevel`. True run joins `2` packet rows, `2` packet fields, `6` flow edges, and `34` source rows; all checks pass. The request field `id` is explicitly filled by `CM_DigitDoorUpLevelFun(self,id)`. Two visible callers exist: `DigitDoorPlayerView` sends the selected character id after checking max level, active state, and local item sufficiency; `DigitDoorPartnerPanel` sends `0` for the bulk/global level-up button after checking `DigitDoor_CharacterLevelCost` through `GetCostInfo`. The response packet class currently declares only `partnerList`; on `msg.code == 0`, visible Lua refreshes all character VOs via `UpdateDDPartnerVos(msg.partnerList)`, raises `Update_Character_Info`, shows `Digit_Door_Tips_27`, and updates the red dot. `SM_DigitDoorUpLevelFun` also passes `msg.partnerVO` to the event, but the exported packet schema/file does not declare `partnerVO`; keep this as a static schema/handler mismatch or stale handler field until runtime evidence says otherwise. Visible resource-cost checks are UI-side guards; the authoritative consume/success comes from the server response.

Additional digitdoor RefDoor pair-flow update: `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_cm_digitdoorrefdoor_sm_digitdoorrefdoor_pair_flow_report.md` drills `CM_DigitDoorRefDoor -> SM_DigitDoorRefDoor`. True run joins `2` packet rows, `3` packet fields, `4` flow edges, and `24` source rows; all checks pass and the previous local-variable alias issue is now correctly attributed to `_CM_DigitDoorRefDoor`. `DigitDoorEntityMgr:UpdatePreCreateDoor(levelTime)` accumulates static door refresh ids into `sendDoorList`, then `CM_DigitDoorRefDoorFun(resourceList)` sends that `resourceList` plus `aliveIdList` built from currently alive partner views. The response carries `doorVOS`; `SM_DigitDoorRefDoorFun` rejects empty lists in debug, otherwise raises `CreateDoor`. `DigitDoorEntityMgr:CreateDoor(msg)` appends `msg.doorVOS`; `CheckCreateDoor` then uses each VO's `resourceId` to find door refresh config and `id` to find `DigitDoor_SkillRefreshEffect`, creates a `DigitDoorBuffEffect` entity, positions it by `side`, and inserts it into fight objects if the configured door type is breakable. Treat this as server-side door-choice confirmation: client proposes candidate resource ids and alive partner ids, server returns the concrete door VO list.

Additional digitdoor GainBuff pair-flow update: `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_cm_digitdoorgainbuff_sm_digitdoorgainbuff_pair_flow_report.md` drills `CM_DigitDoorGainBuff -> SM_DigitDoorGainBuff`. True run joins `2` packet rows, `2` packet fields, `2` flow edges, and `9` source rows; all checks pass. `DigitDoorBuffEffectView:DeadEnd` sends a `buffList` containing the dead door/effect `buffCfg.id` through `CM_DigitDoorGainBuffFun(buffList)`. The response schema also declares `buffList`, but visible `SM_DigitDoorGainBuffFun` only checks `msg.code == 0` and does nothing else. Treat this as a thin server acknowledgement/claim notification for door buff gain; visible local buff application still lives in the skill/effect classes such as `DigitDoorAddBuffEffect`, `DigitDoorAddBuffInEndEffect`, and `DigitDoorBaseSkill:AddBuffData`.

## Hot-Update/Gameplay Findings

BlueStarSea/淬灵域:

- Main config and runtime reports live in `apk_static_index/hot_update_bluestarsea_*.md`.
- The semantic packet graph lives in `hot_update_bluestarsea_protocol_semantics_report.md`; it joins packet id/name, operation, role, NetLogic function, handler, model event, and state writes.
- Client operations are mostly intent-only requests: `faqiId`, `treeId`, `planId`, `starTreeId`, `items`, `times`, `name`, `itemPriority`.
- Server回包 writes final energy/reward/faqi/tree/star/wake/plan state.
- `Star` groups and attr curves have been decoded for `鸿蒙洗髓 / 万源铸本 / 灵海涤魂 / 九霄荡魔 / 星海淬灵`.
- BlueStarSea faze effects connect to `FazeResource/FazeEffectResource`, but many actual algorithms are not visible in Lua and appear server-side or lower-level.

BLLD/百炼轮回:

- Runtime, finish flow, reward, combat, level, and authority-boundary reports exist under `apk_static_index/hot_update_blld_*.md`.
- The semantic packet graph lives in `hot_update_blld_protocol_semantics_report.md`; it joins packet id/name, operation, role, handler, client summary fields, and server state sinks.
- `CM_BlldFinishAndReward` submits local combat summary (`levelId/findReward/success/passRate`), but final display/sync depends on `SM_BlldFinishAndReward` and subsequent sync.

Faze/法则:

- `SM_FazeEffect(fazeId/effectType/num/reason)` is treated as a server rule notification.
- The semantic packet graph lives in `hot_update_faze_protocol_semantics_report.md`; it marks service push packets separately from client-intent request/response pairs and expands `SM_FazeEffect` rule fields.
- Lua mostly renders tips/effects from `fazeId + reason/num`; broad algorithmic semantics are not fully present in current Lua.
- `玄魔大法` is now a focused `Special-GongfaJie -> FazeResource -> FazeEffectResource` case, not an XianShu `SideFeatureJie -> BuffResource` case. True run for `476701` outputs `10` stage rows, all type `804`, and `0` readable Lua usage rows for that type.
- The focused reason probe closes `玄魔大法` reason labels `103/340/649/1254/1256/1257/1265/1269/1270` to `FazeResource.tipStr`; the visible client chain is `SM_FazeEffect -> FazeEffectTip -> FazeType.UpdateFazeEffect`, with no target-specific listener or local type `804` formula found.
- The reason-reuse probe for `1265` confirms that the same `真元自然恢复速度提升` prompt is shared by `玄魔大法(type 804)` and `凤舞九天诀(type 815)`, covering `20` stages across `2` groups. Treat reason labels as shared prompt/reward vocabulary unless a deeper runtime/native path proves unique formula ownership.
- The reason-reuse index finds `31/165` reason codes reused across multiple Gongfa groups. All shared entries cross multiple effect types in the current catalog, so high-frequency reason codes are especially weak evidence for unique local formulas.
- The effect-type index is the stronger static triage surface for this family. It finds `197` effect types; `75` are unique to one Gongfa and carry params/attr payload, while `804` is unique to `玄魔大法` but has no visible payload and only shared prompt vocabulary. This means `804` is a mechanism cluster lead, not a recovered formula.
- The `effect_attr` key index is the strongest static numeric payload surface for Special-GongfaJie/Faze rows that do carry attributes. Current keys are only `14` unique names, and all non-empty payloads parse cleanly as JSON. This is good for wiki display and config ownership, but still not proof that the client is authoritative for those numbers.
- The `effect_params` shape index is the complementary compact-parameter surface. Item-bearing classes are good reward/wiki expansion targets; numeric-only classes are mechanism leads. Neither changes the authority boundary: params are static config payload, not proof that the client computes authoritative outcomes.
- The `effect_params` item-ref report resolves all current `Item|...` references to names and usage distribution. This is directly useful for wiki reward displays and for deciding which params are item/reward payloads versus numeric mechanism leads.
- The payload summary report is now the first file to open for a per-effect-type overview: it points to the right detailed tables for attrs, params, item refs, and reason labels without replacing those details.
- The runtime-sample audit report deliberately separates concrete `SM_FazeEffect` runtime samples from static schema/report references. Current true run finds no runtime sample candidates for `804`, so do not claim exact live trigger frequency or conditions from existing exports alone.
- The global Special-GongfaJie/Faze catalog exports `207` groups and `3407` stage rows for wiki list/detail pages; it also has effect-type and reason summaries for clustering/reuse analysis, plus `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/hot-update/gongfa-special-faze-catalog` for direct UI querying. `/fanxiu/wiki` now renders this in the Gongfa right detail panel as `特殊法则`.
- Reports: `hot_update_faze_authority_boundary_report.md`, `hot_update_faze_effect_catalog_report.md`, `hot_update_faze_effect_lua_usage_report.md`, `hot_update_faze_source_semantics_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_attr_key_index_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_effect_type_index_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_param_item_refs_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_param_shape_index_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_payload_summary_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_runtime_sample_audit_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_catalog_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_focus_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_reason_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_reason_reuse_1265_report.md`, `hot_update_gongfa_special_faze_reason_reuse_index_report.md`.

## Wiki/Catalog State

The Fanxiu wiki goal is to make parsed game objects readable, not just dump raw table rows.

Current important catalog behavior:

- Gongfa cards support grouped duplicate display and right-side full detail expansion.
- Gongfa detail now loads the static `GongFaHomeMake` renderer rows into a game-style `功法效果` panel. It defaults to `include_inactive=false` so the normal view shows active/base text rather than duplicated locked templates.
- XianShu Gongfa detail now loads grouped display-formula samples into a `仙书公式` panel directly below `功法效果`. This uses `XianjieGongfaStar.describe + star params + SideFeatureJie.param` and should be treated as client-visible formula text, not combat authority.
- XianShu Gongfa detail now also loads grouped `BuffResource` parameter semantics into a `仙书机制` panel, with local filtering for mechanism/skill/tag lookup and safe relation jumps when `target_gongfa_id` is known. This is a readable mechanism catalog for timing/lifecycle/cross-table context; it is not proof of complete formula ownership.
- The Gongfa tab now also loads an all-XianShu `仙书机制总览` from the same optional-`gongfa_id` API, so agents/users can search all 55 semantic groups before selecting a specific card.
- The `斗破角色` tab now loads doupotd partner/card data from `parsed_configs/doupotd_catalog`, merging same partner names and showing game-like right-side sections for skill text, combat skills, source/probability panels, card档位, and strengthen rows.
- Rich-text `<color>` tags are converted into readable inline styles in the UI.
- Exact duplicate/grouping work has been done for repeated text IDs and same-name items.
- Raw labels should generally stay hidden unless needed for debugging.
- For “游戏里一样直观”, prefer visual card/detail panels over plain TSV-like listings.

User preference from the UI iteration:

- Show full right-side effect text; avoid nested scroll inside the detail when the page itself can scroll.
- Put useful tag chips near the top only when they help navigation; remove raw/original technical labels from normal view.
- Merge by same display name when exact duplicate entries only differ by `lang.lua:<id>`; allow selecting IDs only when content differs.

## Things Already Learned Not To Overdo

- Cheat Engine against `MuMuNxMain.exe` is not the best route for high-level Unity/IL2CPP game logic. It sees the emulator host process, not a clean C#/Lua object model.
- Clash Verge connection view is useful for domain visibility, not full protocol decoding.
- Not all traffic is HTTP GET/POST. Core gameplay uses binary socket after login/server selection.
- Audio resources are likely Wwise/bank style; do not force generic image/audio parsing. Use external Wwise tools later if audio becomes important.
- Some exported texture previews look meaningless because they are atlases, compressed/packed data, masks, or non-human-facing textures. Do not assume every Unity Texture2D is a useful icon.
- `global-metadata.dat` is useful for names/signatures, but not enough for method bodies. Use Cpp2IL/IL2CPP tooling when control flow matters.
- JADX is useful for DEX/Java shell code such as SDK login callbacks. It does not replace Cpp2IL for Unity IL2CPP method bodies or the hot-update Lua/config analysis.
- `D:\TapTap\Support\android_emulator\download\101000000.dat` is a TapTap/MuMu emulator/update ZIP, not a game resource archive. Do not extract or mine it for Fanxiu assets unless the question is specifically about emulator packaging.

## Maintenance Rule

Update this document when any of these change:

- A new external reverse tool is installed or a tool path changes.
- A new canonical export/report directory is created.
- A major chain is proven, especially login, server-list, socket, resource-download, Lua bridge, or authority boundary.
- CodeYun adds or renames Fanxiu backend modules, API routes, or frontend wiki behavior.

For detailed chronological discoveries, append to:

```text
D:\TapTap\Support\android_emulator\engine\vms\EGTapTap-12.0-1\frxx_analysis_exports\parsed_configs\gongfa_catalog\gongfa_runtime_notes.md
```

For compact agent handoff, update this file.

## Next Best Step

For the current `doupotd` branch, `Change`, `ChangeInit`, the first-battle selector source/static/dynamic registry scans, `Select`, `Effect`, `Pan`, `CanSkill`, `PreInfo`, `SaveHold`, `SaveSkill`, `UpLevel`, `Reset`, `Info`, `Upgrade`, `SkipLevel`, server-only `ActivityEnd/GMBattle/GMClubBattle`, `DouPoCardPanel`, `DouPoCardDraw`, `DouPoCardTaskReward`, `DouPoCardCompose`, `DouPoCardComposeAll`, `DouPoCardMerge`, `DouPoCardJumpPage`, `DouPoCardRedCan`, and `DouPoCardTopQ` are now covered, and the packet coverage inventory reports `0` unclassified visible registrations. `Info` closes the broader activity snapshot entry, `Upgrade` closes the in-run player-grade upgrade boundary, `SkipLevel` closes the one-way skip intent and its `SM_DoupoTDGamePlayer.isSkipLevel` result path, server-only handler review classifies activity-end and GM replay pushes, `DouPoCardPanel` closes the adjacent draw/card panel snapshot, `DouPoCardDraw` closes the actual draw action, `DouPoCardTaskReward` closes the draw-progress reward claim, the two `Compose` pairs close single/batch card composition, `Merge` closes card sorting/collating, `JumpPage` closes visible card-page navigation, `RedCan` closes the server-synced batch-compose availability flag, and `TopQ` closes the card-mode max-quality/max-star display cache. `PreInfo/UpLevel` both show doupotd schema/handler mismatches around fields copied from or still present in towerdefense, while `CanSkill/SaveHold/SaveSkill/Reset` are clean on schema/handler but missing visible doupotd UI callers. Static config-to-wiki enrichment for doupotd partner/card data now exists as `parsed_configs/doupotd_catalog` and the `斗破角色` wiki tab, including `DrawCard / ComposeProgress / ComposeBook / ComposeCardQuality` source/probability panels. `CharacterSkillInfo` is now linked to doupotd timeline/effect classes by `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_skill_timeline_link_report.md`; `buffId` values are now linked to `BuffEffect` rows and `DoupoTDBuff*` dispatch classes by `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_buff_effect_link_report.md`; class-level flags/evidence are summarized by `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_buff_class_semantics_report.md`; class-level control flow is summarized by `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_buff_class_flow_report.md` and rendered in the wiki; class flow versus visible packet authority is summarized by `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_buff_authority_boundary_report.md`; `effectVO/bossVoList` nested summary semantics are summarized by `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_effect_gameplayer_summary_report.md`; `rewardResults/passLevelVOS` result consumption is summarized by `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_gameplayer_result_report.md`; static reward config, `RewardResult.type/code/amount/extraMark` item-resolution semantics, `MonsterGroup.drops -> Drop.ItemTeam` candidate expansion, and `StoreContentBag` visual/runtime boundary are summarized by `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_reward_config_report.md`, `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_reward_result_resolution_report.md`, `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_monster_drop_resolution_report.md`, and `lua_lscript_module_doupotd_store_bag_visual_report.md`. The wiki `斗破奖励` tab now includes the concise `结算边界` explanation; the monster-drop report confirms `Drop.ItemTeam` is static candidate/shared config while actual drop units and pickup settlement are server-provided; the store-bag report confirms `Drop.StoreContentBag` is visual metadata resolved by server-provided `BagUnitVO.bagModel`, with only 10/31 doupotd monsters directly matching by static id/base id. The next doupotd reward step should be runtime calibration only if a privacy-filtered `SM_DropObjects/SM_PickUp`, `SM_BagObjects/SM_BagInfo/SM_OpenBag`, or `SM_DoupoTDGamePlayer.rewardResults` sample is available; otherwise switch to another narrow static branch instead of repeating per-Buff state, static reward-config, ItemTeam, or StoreContentBag id-match scans.

Latest package addendum: `101000000.dat` is now classified by `taptap_download_dat_package_report.md` as an emulator/update package, not the game hot-update resource payload. Do not revisit it for Fanxiu resource extraction; continue resource analysis from `frxx_game_files`, `filelist.csv`, `resource_manifest_diff_report.md`, and exported `by_source/lscripts`.

Latest digitdoor addendum: first-layer `digitdoor` static reports now exist (`lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_surface_report.md`, `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_netlogic_flow_report.md`, `lua_lscript_module_digitdoor_protocol_schema_report.md`), and `ReadyFight`, `Unlock`, `GamePlayer`, `StartGame`, `RefreshWave`, `UpLevel`, `RefDoor`, `GainBuff`, plus report/server-only endpoints are now drilled. ReadyFight is a scene-ready snapshot boundary; Unlock is a character-state refresh boundary; GamePlayer is the main settlement boundary; StartGame is the pre-battle lineup confirmation boundary; RefreshWave is the mid-run progress synchronization boundary; UpLevel is the character progression/update boundary; RefDoor is the door/buff-effect spawn confirmation boundary; GainBuff is a thin door-buff claim/ack boundary; `CM_DigitDoorReport` is a PVP combat report upload, while ActivityEnd/GMBattle are server-only finish/replay triggers. Next useful DigitDoor target is no longer packet coverage but deeper config/runtime cataloging: character/door/buff config joins, result/reward static config joins, or UI wiki integration if this module becomes user-facing. Do not treat the old `CM_DigitDoorUpLevel.resourceList/aliveIdList` mismatch as real; it was a local-variable alias artifact and the analyzer now follows `GetMessageFromPools`.

Latest digitdoor catalog addendum: `backend/core/fanxiu_digitdoor_catalog.py` and `parsed_configs/digitdoor_catalog/*` now provide the first structured config catalog. It joins `CharacterMainInfo` to `CharacterLevel`, `CharacterSkillShow`, `CharacterSkillInfo`, `SkillEnhanceEffect`, and `SkillRefreshEffect`; it links door-effect skill ids to `SkillEnhanceEffect.id`, plus same-id show/logic rows when present; it keeps `SkillEnhance` separate because its `charId` is not the same namespace as `CharacterMainInfo.id`. The earlier `233` unresolved door-skill refs are now resolved by `SkillEnhanceEffect`, so the next narrow target is wiring the digitdoor character catalog into `/fanxiu/wiki` or drilling how `SkillEnhanceEffect.buffId/ext*` fields are consumed by DigitDoor skill classes.

Latest digitdoor BuffEffect usage addendum: `build_fanxiu_digitdoor_buff_effect_usage_probe` now generates `parsed_configs/digitdoor_catalog/buff_effect_usage_report.md` plus config/type/field/Lua hit/topic TSVs. True run confirms `220` BuffEffect rows, `18` SkillBuffType values, and `662` Lua evidence hits. Runtime chain: `BuffEffect.type -> DigitDoorType.SkillBuffType -> DigitDoorType.BuffPath -> Buff class`, then `DigitDoorPartnerView:AddBuff` handles stacking/refresh and instantiation, while `DigitDoorBuffBase:InitData/Start/Update` owns common fields such as target/trigger/duration/timeline/passive/layer limit. Type distribution is dominated by `AddAttr=96`, `Shield=28`, `SkillDamageStrength=23`, `ReduceCD=13`, `BeHit=13`, and `HitAddBuff=12`. Field distribution highlights `add_attr=103`, `timeline_id=153`, `passive=44`, `shield=28`, `buff_amplify=27`, and `target_buff_check=15`. New CodeYun API: `POST /api/fanxiu/resources/digitdoor/buff-effect-usage-probe`. Latest digitdoor Buff class formula addendum: `build_fanxiu_digitdoor_buff_class_formula_probe` now generates `parsed_configs/digitdoor_catalog/buff_class_formula_report.md` plus field/source/Lua-hit/topic TSVs. True run confirms `220` BuffEffect rows, `10` formula fields, and `349` Lua formula evidence hits. Key formulas: `addAttr` becomes `cfg value * (1 + strengthVal * 0.0001)`, aggregates as `sum(value * layer)`, and is displayed as percent by `* 0.01`; `shield` becomes `cfg.shield * (1 + strengthVal * 0.0001)` and `shieldValue=maxHp*sum(shieldRatio)*0.0001`; `damage` also receives the same `strengthVal` multiplier before entering `AddDamageResult`; nonnegative `duration` and `interval` are millisecond configs converted to seconds; `decreaseCd` subtracts milliseconds from ultra-skill cooldown; trigger buffs roll `1..10000` against `triggerPercent`; `hpJudge` is `cfg.hpJudge * 0.0001`. New CodeYun API: `POST /api/fanxiu/resources/digitdoor/buff-class-formula-probe`. Validation after this addendum: `uv run pytest backend/tests/test_fanxiu_resources.py -q` is `165 passed`; `uv run python -m py_compile backend\core\fanxiu_digitdoor_catalog.py backend\api\fanxiu_resources.py backend\tests\test_fanxiu_resources.py` passes; current Fanxiu resource route count is `208`.

Latest digitdoor partner-attribute formatter addendum: `build_fanxiu_digitdoor_partner_attribute_formatter_probe` now writes `parsed_configs/digitdoor_catalog/partner_attribute_formatter_report.md`, `partner_attribute_formatter_sources.tsv`, `partner_attribute_formatter_fields.tsv`, and `partner_attribute_formatter_evidence.tsv`, with CodeYun API `POST /api/fanxiu/resources/digitdoor/partner-attribute-formatter-probe`. True run scans `10` source files and emits `26` field rows plus `146` evidence rows. The surface is a debug/editor display path: `DigitDoorSceneView` has `24` `LuaGlobal.IsDebugBuild` guard hits and `3` `TestTimeTF` output hits, while `SkillEditorBridge.GetDigitDoorPartnerAttributes` is the native/editor bridge. It still reads real runtime entity views: base attributes come from `DigitDoorEntityData` getters, and buff attributes aggregate live `BuffDic` AddAttr/Shield/Injure data. Key formulas: AddAttr config values become `value * (1 + strengthVal * 0.0001)`, aggregate as `sum(GetAddExtBattleAttr(type) * layer)`, and display as percent by `* 0.01`; shield value is `maxHp * sum(GetShieldRatio()) * 0.0001`; Injure displays as `sum(GetInjuredValue() * layer) * 0.01`. The formatter has `0` `SM_DigitDoorStartGame` / `skillVos` / `DDSkillVo` references, so it explains local attribute inspection without closing StartGame `skillVos`. Validation after this update: py_compile passes, focused pytest passes, full `uv run pytest backend/tests/test_fanxiu_resources.py -q` is `167 passed`, Fanxiu router count is now `240`, codeyun dev server was restarted, and the real POST API smoke returns `safe_static_debug_formatter_boundary=true`.

Local combat consumption is now clearer: `DigitDoorFightComponent:AddDamageResult` reads `GetAttackerFinalAttr/GetDefenseFinalAttr`, Injure, Shield, HP apply, and HurtData projection; attacker AddAttr consumes `Attack/CriticalRate/CriticalDamage/IncreaseDamage/AddDamage/SkillDamage`, defender AddAttr consumes `AntiCritical/ReduceDamage`, and `DigitDoorBaseSkill:CalculateSkillSpeed` consumes `AttackSpeed` with `atkSpeed * (1 / (1 + extAtkSpeed * 0.0001))`. Shield is consumed and mutated before HP damage applies; Injure multiplies damage against DigitDoor bots. `MaxHp` appears only under `LuaGlobal.IsUnityEditor` in `DigitDoorBuffAddAttr`, so keep it as editor-only on the visible Lua surface. Latest digitdoor GamePlayer settlement-boundary addendum: `build_fanxiu_digitdoor_gameplayer_settlement_probe` now writes `parsed_configs/digitdoor_catalog/gameplayer_settlement_report.md`, `gameplayer_settlement_fields.tsv`, and `gameplayer_settlement_evidence.tsv`, with CodeYun API `POST /api/fanxiu/resources/digitdoor/gameplayer-settlement-probe`. HP loss is additionally capped by `PVPDamageLimit`, initialized from winner/loser front/back limit pairs and periodically restored by `DigitDoorPartnerView:UpdatePVPDamageLimit`. Winner protection compares winner/loser current HP totals, adjusts `PVPWinnerReduceDamage`, and after the time threshold repeatedly adds `PVPWinnerExtAttack`. `DigitDoorPVPSceneView:GetAttr` emits `PVPATTACK/PVPINCREASE/PVPREDUCE` into PVP report snapshots, but server acceptance/validation remains outside this static Lua surface. It scans `16828` Lua logic files, skips `11786` generated message schema files, and finds `690` code-reference hits across `199` logic-hit files, persisted as `473` capped evidence rows. All `60` observed protocols have visible Lua logic references; `28` are classified as visible server-packet handlers, `16` as visible client-sender surfaces, `3` as MessagePool registered without visible `*Fun`, and `13` as require-only. High-signal follow-up families include `SM_RewardResult`, `SM_PracticeCollect`, `SM_ActivityRankSync`, scene/map packets, seat/veins packets, Yunmeng PK, BLLD, and activity rank flows. Conclusion: the old pcap cannot prove DoupoTD PVP report runtime behavior, but it can seed general packet -> Lua handler -> model/UI follow-up for the packets it actually contains. Persisted outputs remain code-reference metadata only; no payload values are exported. Validation after this update: py_compile passes, focused pcap-observed-Lua-handler pytest passes, full `uv run pytest backend\tests\test_fanxiu_resources.py -q` is `207 passed`, route-table import via `backend.app` shows `803` routes and all five pcap routes exist, `git diff --check` only reports LF-to-CRLF warnings, and the true builder run returns `confirmed=true`.

Latest old-pcap Venis/UnionVeins seat-role chain addendum: `build_fanxiu_doupotd_pcap_observed_venis_union_seat_role_chain_probe` now writes `parsed_configs/doupotd_catalog/doupotd_pcap_observed_venis_union_seat_role_chain_report.md`, packets/chain/functions/evidence/consumers TSVs, and JSON, with CodeYun API `POST /api/fanxiu/resources/doupotd/pcap-observed-venis-union-seat-role-chain-probe`. True run starts from 12 old-pcap observed Venis/UnionVeins seat-social packets that the generic handler summary had all marked `require_only`: `CM/SM_VeinsLastLeaveSeatInfo(87209/87210)`, `CM/SM_SyncVeinsRoleInfo(87211/87212)`, `CM/SM_VeinsSelfSeat(87226/87227)`, `CM/SM_UnionVeinsLastLeaveSeatInfo(93510/93511)`, `CM/SM_SyncUnionVeinsRoleInfo(93512/93513)`, and `CM/SM_UnionVeinsSelfSeat(93527/93528)`, each with `observed_packet_count=2`. The direct chain shows this is a classifier blind spot rather than a missing handler: `VenisNetLogic` / `UnionVenisNetLogic` require the concrete packet classes, register them through shared aliases (`_CM_LastLeaveSeatInfo`, `_SM_SyncVenisRoleInfo`, `_CM_SelfSeat`) and inline callback wrappers, send `CM_SyncVenisRoleInfo`, `CM_LastLeaveSeatInfo`, `CM_SelfSeat`, and clean-last-leave requests through `SocketManager`, then dispatch successful SM responses to `SyncRoleInfo(msg)`, `CheckLastLeaveSeatInfo(msg)`, and `SetMySeatVO(msg.seatVO)`. `VenisData` / `UnionVenisData` cache role info (`roomId`, `seatId`, `leftListenTime`, `rewardIds`, server/group/buff fields, plus UnionVeins `battleId`), last-leave reminders (`leaveBySelf`, `listenTime`, reward-window cleanup), and local seat VO state, then raise `MySeatUpdate` / `MySeatIdUpdate` and red-dot/update events consumed by room, scene, seat, reward, search, and item views. The true report records `1` Venis and `1` UnionVenis text dir, `86` scanned Lua files, `24` packet schema files, `12` packet-index registration rows, `287` function rows, `481` focused evidence rows, and `172` consumers; evidence includes `24` packet registration rows, `38` client request-surface rows, `22` server response handler rows, `51` role-info cache rows, `30` last-leave cache rows, `80` self-seat cache rows, `116` view-state consumer rows, and `120` packet-schema rows. Conclusion: this is concrete Venis/UnionVeins social/seat state synchronization evidence and a correction to the generic `require_only` classifier result, not DoupoTD PVP report evidence, not combat formula authority, and not a payload decoder; it exports source-code metadata rather than live role, room, seat, reward, union, server, timestamp, account, or payload values. Validation after this update: py_compile passes, focused `venis_union_seat_role` pytest passes, full `uv run pytest backend\tests\test_fanxiu_resources.py -q` is `235 passed`, route-table import via `backend.app` shows `921` routes and the Venis/UnionVeins seat-role chain route exists, and the true builder run returns `confirmed=true`.

Latest static visual asset catalog addendum: `build_fanxiu_static_visual_catalog` now writes `parsed_configs/visual_catalog/static_visual_catalog_report.md`, `visual_asset_catalog.tsv`, `icon_logo_gallery.html`, `atlas_sprite_catalog.tsv`, `atlas_bundle_summary.tsv`, `icon_logo_sprite_catalog.tsv`, `apk_visual_assets.tsv`, `apk_icon_logo_assets.tsv`, `sprite_usage_refs.tsv`, `sprite_usage_summary.tsv`, and JSON, with CodeYun API `POST /api/fanxiu/resources/visual/static-catalog`. True run over the local resource/APK exports parses all `181` `atlasnew` Unity bundles with `0` bundle errors, indexes and exports all `13,108` atlas sprite PNGs, scans/copies all `579` unpacked APK image assets, and builds a full manifest of `13,687` browser-viewable static images. The legacy focused subset is preserved as `icon_logo_sprite_catalog.tsv` with `7,944` target icon/title/logo sprites and `apk_icon_logo_assets.tsv` with `289` APK logo/icon/splash/SDK UI rows. Full asset groups are `icon=6544`, `image=3894`, `text=1527`, `sprite=1270`, and `apk=452`; category counts are `sprite=5164`, `item_or_ui_icon=3812`, `title_label=1527`, `skill_icon=1365`, `fashion_icon=726`, `head_portrait=464`, `apk_image=290`, `sdk_ui=162`, `apk_icon=118`, `buff_icon=40`, `ability_icon=9`, `apk_logo=6`, `apk_splash=3`, and `logo=1`. The usage back-reference pass scans `28,722` config/Lua text files with `0` read errors, records `110,280` sprite references across `6,279` target sprites, and is not truncated; top referenced sprites include `common_buff_icon_0003`, `common_buff_icon_0016`, `common_buff_icon_0010`, `common_buff_icon_0002`, `common_buff_icon_0011`, `icon_charge_0001`, `icon_charge_0002`, `skill_buff_0030`, and `icon_item_0486`. This is static asset metadata, cropped image export, and exact string reference indexing only: no runtime state, traffic payload values, account data, or APK/resource mutation. Latest static Wwise MP3 export addendum: installed `vgmstream-cli` at `D:\tools\vgmstream\nightly\vgmstream-cli.exe` and reused the existing Scoop `ffmpeg` at `C:\Users\kzche\scoop\shims\ffmpeg.EXE` to convert Wwise `.bnk` embedded `.wem` payloads into browser-playable MP3. `build_fanxiu_wwise_mp3_export` now writes `parsed_configs/audio_catalog/wwise_mp3_manifest.tsv`, `wwise_mp3_export_report.md`, `wwise_mp3_export.json`, and MP3 files under `parsed_configs/audio_catalog/mp3`, with CodeYun API `POST /api/fanxiu/resources/wwise/mp3-export`, manifest endpoint `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/wwise/mp3-manifest`, media endpoint `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/audio/media?path=...`, and CLI wrapper `uv run python scripts/export_fanxiu_wwise_mp3.py --mp3-quality 4`. Latest Fanxiu wiki resource gallery UI addendum: `/fanxiu/wiki` now has first-class `图片` and `音乐` tabs next to `道具/活动/功法`. Backend adds `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/visual/manifest` and `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/visual/media` over `parsed_configs/visual_catalog`, while the existing MP3 manifest/media endpoints back the music tab. The visual tab now reads the full `13,687` image manifest, not only icon/logo targets, and exposes asset group filters `全部/图标/标题/大图/切片/APK`; `query=游戏公告` is expanded through visual query aliases and currently returns `3` rows (`2` APK notice SDK assets and `1` atlas title-label row `doupold_ggl_zw_0001`). The audio tab lists the `3,180` MP3 rows and renders a native `<audio>` player; browser check with `?tab=audio&q=amb_battle` resolves the `ambient` entry `302667933` and shows a `0:34` player. Validation after this update: py_compile passes, focused visual/audio pytest passes, frontend `typecheck` and `build` pass, route-table import shows visual/audio manifest/media routes, HTTP checks show `visual/manifest?query=游戏公告` returns `filtered=3` and `asset_group=image` returns `3894`, and Playwright screenshot checks confirm both tabs are visible and populated.

Latest Fanxiu wiki visual similarity search addendum: the `图片` tab supports direct paste image search with no upload/button entry: when the visual tab is active, pasting a screenshot/image triggers the search immediately, and the resulting query strip shows a clickable thumbnail that opens the larger image preview. The frontend wraps that pasted image and sends it to `POST /api/fanxiu/resources/visual/similarity` with multipart field `image`. `build_fanxiu_static_visual_catalog` writes `parsed_configs/visual_catalog/visual_similarity_index.tsv` with `phash-8` and `dhash-8` values for each exported visual row; after the full-image expansion this index covers `13,687` rows with `0` hash errors and is about `5.92 MB`. Query flow decodes only the pasted image, normalizes alpha/uniform borders, computes its pHash/dHash once, optionally filters by visual asset group, then ranks existing rows by cheap integer Hamming distances plus a small aspect-ratio bonus. The API returns the normal visual manifest row shape plus `similarity_rank`, `similarity_percent`, `phash_distance`, `dhash_distance`, and `aspect_similarity`; the frontend shows ranks/similarity in the left list and detail panel. Current real-data first lazy index build over the old `8,233` image subset took about `52s`; full rebuild now happens during the static visual catalog pass. Validation after this update: py_compile passes, focused static visual pytest passes, frontend `typecheck` and `build` pass, route-table import confirms `/api/fanxiu/resources/visual/similarity`, and HTTP multipart search against the running backend returns the expected ranked list.

Latest Fanxiu wiki tab hierarchy addendum: `/fanxiu/wiki` now keeps high-weight catalog and reverse-entry tabs at the top level: `道具`, `活动`, `功法`, `灵界词条`, `抓包`, and trailing `逆向资料`. The lower-weight reverse/static/technical catalogs are preserved as second-level tabs under `逆向资料`: `图片`, `素材`, `音乐`, `数字门角色`, `数字门关卡`, `数字门强化`, `斗破角色`, `斗破奖励`, and `协议`. Internal route state remains leaf-based (`tab=visual`, `tab=asset`, `tab=audio`, `tab=digitdoor`, etc.), so existing deep links still open the correct second-level tab while reverse-static tabs resolve the top-level active tab to `逆向资料`; `tab=packet` opens top-level `抓包` directly. Validation after this UI hierarchy update: frontend `typecheck` and `build` pass, and Playwright screenshots over `?tab=visual&q=logo` and `?tab=digitdoor` confirm the new two-level navigation and populated content.

Latest static Unity asset catalog addendum: `build_fanxiu_static_asset_catalog` now writes `parsed_configs/asset_catalog/static_asset_catalog_report.md`, `static_asset_catalog.tsv`, and `static_asset_catalog.json`, with CodeYun API `POST /api/fanxiu/resources/asset/static-catalog` and manifest endpoint `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/asset/manifest`. This catalog intentionally covers the non-picture side of the resource graph: Unity bundles and exported metadata paths for `model`, `effect`, `uieffect`, `ui`, `scenepart`, `wholescene`, `animationclip`, `animatorcontroller`, and `playable`, grouped for the wiki as `模型`, `特效`, `UI`, `场景`, and `动画`. True run indexes `19,548` static asset rows totaling about `6.36 GB`: `effect=8543`, `model=3691`, `animation=3668`, `ui=3092`, and `scene=554`; source-kind counts include `effect=4675`, `uieffect=3868`, `model=3691`, `ui=3092`, `playable=2164`, `animationclip=1017`, `animatorcontroller=487`, `scenepart=382`, and `wholescene=172`. Chinese query aliases were added for common in-game names, so `asset/manifest?query=游戏公告` currently returns UI/effect rows, while `asset/manifest?query=飞剑&asset_group=model` returns model rows. The wiki `逆向资料` section now includes a `素材` tab backed by this endpoint, with type filtering and detail panels for path/source/category/size plus any old mesh/material/texture/animation/UI-node detail rows that can be joined. Boundary: this is full static bundle inventory and metadata search, not a 3D model renderer, prefab runtime reconstruction, OCR text extractor, or live UI screenshot decomposer; deep mesh/material preview should be done as a later targeted pass by category. Validation after this update: py_compile passes, focused `static_asset` pytest passes, frontend `typecheck` and `build` pass, HTTP `asset/manifest` returns the true counts above, and the dev server is running with the new tab available.

Latest static Unity asset object-type addendum: the `素材` catalog is no longer only a path/bundle index. `build_fanxiu_static_asset_catalog(parse_unity_objects=True)` now parses Unity object handles for each indexed bundle and writes frontend-visible fields into `static_asset_catalog.tsv`: `visible_data_type`, `unity_object_count`, `unity_object_types`, `unity_primary_type`, `unity_named_objects`, `unity_script_names`, `unity_read_error_count`, `unity_parse_status`, and `unity_parse_error`. True full run parsed `19,547 / 19,548` rows successfully with `1` parse error/empty edge case. Inferred visible data types are `particle_effect=8168`, `ui_prefab=3128`, `skinned_mesh=2565`, `timeline_config=2164`, `animator_controller=1427`, `animation_clip=1044`, `mesh_model=434`, `scene_prefab=311`, `unity_asset=258`, and `script_config=48`. The frontend now shows these object types in the `素材` list/detail, including object-type counts such as `GameObject/RectTransform/ParticleSystem/Material/Mesh/AnimationClip/AnimatorController` and name/script samples such as `EffectRoot`, `EffectUI`, or prefab object names. The broad `ggl` alias was narrowed to `doupold_ggl`/`ggl_zw` because object-name search made it falsely match strings like `fudengglow`; current HTTP `asset/manifest?query=游戏公告` returns `31` rows with `ui_prefab=25`, `particle_effect=5`, and `animator_controller=1`. Validation after this update: py_compile passes, focused `static_asset or static_visual` pytest passes, frontend `typecheck` passes, and the restarted dev server returns the new object-type fields.

Latest static Unity asset preview addendum: `素材` rows originally included lazy low-level bundle preview URLs backed by `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/asset/preview?path=...` and `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/asset/preview-manifest?path=...`. Preview cache is `v3`: if a bundle contains embedded `Texture2D` or `Sprite`, each decoded original image is written as its own PNG with name/size/path text in HTML outside the image; it no longer builds a contact-sheet PNG with labels. This remains useful for reverse-engineering evidence, but it is no longer the wiki-facing default because model diffuse/normal/material textures are often not a meaningful finished asset.

Latest static semantic visual asset addendum: the `/fanxiu/wiki` `素材` tab now exposes only the business-semantic catalog in the frontend; the old `原始包/raw` frontend switch was removed. Backend `catalog_view=raw|gallery` remains available for direct reverse-engineering API calls, but UI users see `catalog_view=semantic` only. `static_asset_semantic_catalog.tsv` is now cache version `v2` and joins static bundle references with finished sprite images from `parsed_configs/visual_catalog/visual_asset_catalog.tsv`: `Model.head/lieHead/lie`, `Monster.modelId -> Model` visual refs, `Skill.icon`, `BuffResource.icon`, and `GongfaSkill.icon`. Semantic rows carry `semantic_visual_count`, `semantic_visual_names`, `semantic_visual_categories`, and `semantic_visual_media_paths`; the API converts these to `/api/fanxiu/resources/visual/media?...` URLs and uses the first one as the row thumbnail. For semantic rows the frontend no longer auto-opens raw Unity bundle previews, so details show `业务图片` rather than texture atlases, normal maps, prefab diagrams, or object-summary placeholders. The catalog also collapses `GongfaSkill` same-name/same-icon rows and same-name `Skill` rows into one business entry with `semantic_variant_count` / `semantic_variant_refs`, because these tables store one row per stage/numeric variant. Real check after rebuild: semantic catalog count is `21,164`; query `东乙枯荣经` now returns `3` rows instead of a long repeated list: `东乙枯荣经` (`26` GongfaSkill variants), `圣·东乙枯荣经` (`163` variants), and one grouped Skill support row (`4` variants). Query `阴冥飞兽` returns model/monster rows with `head_icon_4414` and `npcicon_pic_0004`; query `1000601_1` returns `buff:1000601 / 眩晕` with `common_buff_icon_0012` and linked support resources. Boundary: this is still a static config-to-visual semantic index, not Unity runtime rendering with shaders, animations, external atlas dependency resolution, or interactive 3D playback. Validation after this update: py_compile passes, focused static asset pytest passes including semantic Model visual fixture coverage, frontend `typecheck` and `build` pass, and direct real-data checks confirm business images are present for model/monster/buff/gongfa semantic rows.

Latest static semantic business-source addendum: `static_asset_semantic_catalog.tsv` is now cache version `v4` and also indexes frontend-visible business rows from `Item`, `OpenFunction`, `Activity`, and `ActivityGift`, joined through their icon/sprite fields. The wiki `素材` type facets now include `功能`, `道具`, `活动`, and `礼包`, ahead of model/monster/skill groups, while the frontend still stays on the single semantic catalog. A focused bridge row records the Lua-backed small-bottle icon: `function:small_bottle / 小绿瓶 / 修炼入口` uses visual `common_icon_1002`, with evidence from `BookMainPanel.lua:163`, bottleworld `ConfigValue[70]`, and OpenFunction rows `8008/15000`. Query aliases now tolerate `绿品/绿瓶/小绿瓶`, `炼丹炉`, and `首充`; `炼丹炉` is intentionally mapped to `丹炉/liandanlu`-style model terms instead of broad `炼丹/dan`, so it does not rank unrelated “炼丹基金” rows first. Exact query relevance sorting was added so `功法经验` ranks the `Item` row before broader `功法心得` matches and `首充豪礼` ranks the exact OpenFunction row first. `v4` also collapses exact duplicate `Model`/`Monster` semantic rows when they share the same business image and linked resource signature, keeping all original IDs in `semantic_variant_refs`; this removes repeated config IDs such as model rows that only differ by ID but point to the same ResPath/AniController/head. Variant IDs now participate in query ranking, so searching a folded model ID can still surface the folded row. True rebuild result after this collapse is `20,929` semantic rows. Real checks: `query=绿品` and `query=小绿瓶` both return `小绿瓶 / 修炼入口` with `common_icon_1002` first; `query=功法经验` returns `item:1004 / 功法经验` with `icon_item_0050` first; `query=首充豪礼` returns `function:94003 / 首充豪礼` with `mainui_icon_0737` and `common_function_icon_0006` first. Validation: py_compile passes, focused `static_asset` pytest passes, and frontend `typecheck` passes.

Latest Fanxiu wiki independent audio playback addendum: the `音乐` detail panel now exposes a compact `独立播放` action next to the embedded audio player. It opens a standalone playback window rather than another Vue route, so Vite HMR or CodeYun frontend hot reloads do not reset that separate playback tab. New manifest rows include `player_url` backed by `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/audio/player?path=...`, which renders a custom dark audio page with a large range progress bar, explicit elapsed/duration labels, a play/pause button, volume control, and the guarded MP3 media endpoint underneath. If the current dev backend has not hot-reloaded the new `player_url` field yet, the frontend falls back to a generated Blob HTML player that uses the same enlarged controls and rewrites proxied `/api/...` media URLs from `127.0.0.1:5173` to `127.0.0.1:8000`. Validation after this update: backend `py_compile` passes, focused Wwise MP3 pytest passes, frontend `typecheck` and `build` pass, and the standalone-player markup avoids Vue SFC `</script>` parser termination by escaping the embedded script close tag.

Latest Fanxiu wiki audio kind filter addendum: the `音乐` tab now has an explicit `类型` filter row backed by the MP3 manifest `kind` field. The exposed filters are `全部`, `BGM`, `AMB`, `UI`, and `音效`; `AMB` maps to backend kind `ambient`, and the backend also accepts `kind=amb` as an alias. The manifest response now includes `stats.kinds`, `stats.query_kinds`, and `stats.query_total`, so the UI can show type counts for the current search context while filtering through `GET /api/fanxiu/resources/wwise/mp3-manifest?kind=...`. Current true counts are `audio=2998`, `bgm=89`, `ui=57`, and `ambient=36`. Validation after this update: py_compile passes, focused Wwise MP3 pytest passes, frontend `typecheck` and `build` pass, API checks show `kind=bgm` returns `89` and `kind=amb` returns `36`, and Playwright screenshot confirms the audio kind filter row renders.
